I just can't seem to sit still and take it easy.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed your thoughts racing or feeling like you don’t need as much sleep lately?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. My mind isn't really racing... I mean, I'm always thinking about the next chore I need to do around the house, but it's not out of control or anything. \n\nAnd as for sleep, no, I still need my regular amount of rest. I sleep just fine at night, same as always. It's really just during the day that I feel like I have to keep moving and can't settle down.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"

    "role": "user",
    "content": "Return the JSON now."
  }

So, no changes there.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any recent periods where you felt unusually energetic, excited, or upbeat for days at a time, or where you were more talkative or confident than usual?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had anything like that. I don't get unusually excited or energetic for days on end, and I'm not really more talkative than normal. I'm just my regular self, really.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period lasting several days or more where you felt extremely upbeat, irritable, or so energetic that others noticed a change in your behavior?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't ever had anything like that. Like I said, I don't get those kinds of extreme highs or bursts of energy where I'm acting out of character for days. I mean, I get restless, sure, but I've never had a time where people were pointing out that I was acting overly upbeat or irritable like that. Just my normal day-to-day.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when you felt you needed much less sleep than usual and still felt energetic or wide awake, even if you only slept a few hours?\n\nPatient: No, never. Like I mentioned before, if I don't get my normal amount of sleep, I'm dragging the next day. I've never had a time where I could just sleep a couple of hours and feel ready to go. I need my rest.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any days recently where you felt down or where things just didn’t seem enjoyable, even if only for a short time?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I have normal days where maybe I'm a little bored or tired, but I haven't felt down or depressed. I still enjoy the things I usually do. So, no, nothing like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you don’t feel much pleasure or enjoyment, even when good things happen?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. If something good happens, I feel good about it. I still get plenty of enjoyment out of the things I usually like to do. That hasn't been a problem for me.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. I'm just my regular self, really.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period lasting several days or more where you felt extremely upbeat, irritable, or so energetic that others noticed a change in your behavior?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't ever had anything like that. Like I said, I don't get those kinds of extreme highs or bursts of energy where I'm acting out of character for days. I mean, I get restless, sure, but I've never had a time where people were pointing out that I was acting overly upbeat or irritable like that. Just my normal day-to-day.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a time when you felt you needed much less sleep than usual and still felt energetic or wide awake, even if you only slept a few hours?\n\nPatient: No, never. I need my rest.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any days recently where you felt down or where things just didn’t seem enjoyable, even if only for a short time?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I have normal days where maybe I'm a little bored or tired, but I haven't felt down or depressed. I still enjoy the things I usually do. So, no, nothing like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you don’t feel much pleasure or enjoyment, even when good things happen?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. That hasn't been a problem for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any days recently where you didn’t take pleasure in things you usually enjoy, even for a short time?\n\nPatient: No, like I just said, I still enjoy my regular activities. I know you're asking it a few different ways, but my answer is still the same. I haven't lost interest in any of my usual stuff. I still like doing whatever I normally do just fine, so that hasn't been an issue at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that your movements have slowed down, or that you speak more slowly than usual, to the point where others might notice?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't noticed anything like that. I feel pretty normal when it comes to how I move and speak.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any trouble concentrating, making decisions, or thinking clearly lately?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I don't really get down in the dumps for weeks on end, and my sleep and appetite are pretty steady.
